 430\t\n   431\t### \u56db\u3001\u540c\u4e49\u8bcd\u6269\u5c55\u4e0e\u67e5\u8be2\u6539\u5199\n   432\t\n   433\t**LLM\u9a71\u52a8\u7684\u67e5\u8be2\u6269\u5c55**\uff1a\n   434\t| \u65b9\u6cd5 | MS 2024\uff09\uff1a\n   447\t- MIRAGE\u57fa\u51c6\uff087,663\u95ee\u9898\uff0c5\u6570\u636e\u96c6\uff09\n   448\t- \u63d0\u53476\u79cdLLM\u51c6\u786e\u7387\u6700\u9ad8**18%**\n   449\t\n   450\t**OG-RAG**\uff08EMNLP 2025\uff09\uff1a\n   451\t- \u672c\u4f53\u951a\u5b9a\u68c0\u7d22\u589e\u5f3a\u751f\u6210\n   452\t- \u8d85\u56fe\u8868\u793a\u9886\u57df\u6587\u6863\n   453\t\n   454\t| \u6307\u6807 | \u63d0\u5347 |\n   455\t|------|------|\n   456\t| \u4e8b\u5b9e\u53ec\u56de | **+55%** |\n   457\t| \u54cd\u5e94\u6b63\u786e\u6027 | **+40%** |\n   458\t| \u7528\u6237\u9a8c\u8bc1\u901f\u5ea6 | **+30%** |\n   459\t\n   460\t### \u516d\u3001RAG\u6574\u5408\u67b6\u6784\u63a8\u8350\n   461\t\n   462\t```python\n   463\tclass LexicalSemanticRAG:\n   464\t    \"\"\"\u8bcd\u6c47\u8bed\u4e49\u589e\u5f3a\u7684RAG\u7cfb\u7edf\"\"\"\n   465\t    \n   466\t    def __init__(self):\n   467\t        # 1. Dense Retriever\n   468\t        self.dense_retriever = BGEEmbedding()  # \u6216 SimCSE\n   469\t        \n   470\t        # 2. Lexical Re-ranker\n   471\t        self.lexical_ranker = BM25WithWordNet()\n   472\t        \n   473\t        # 3. NLI Filter\n   474\t        self.nli_filter = DeBERTaV3MNLI()\n   475\t        \n   476\t        # 4. Knowledge Graph Alignment\n   477\t        self.kg_aligner = ConceptNetLinker()  # \u6216 UMLS\n   478\t        \n   479\t        # 5. Generator\n   480\t        self.generator = LLM()\n   481\t    \n   482\t    def retrieve_and_generate(self, query):\n   483\t        # Step 1: \u540c\u4e49\u8bcd\u6269\u5c55\n   484\t        expanded_query = self.expand_with_wordnet(query)\n   485\t        \n   486\t        # Step 2: Dense\u68c0\u7d22\n   487\t        candidates = self.dense_retriever.retrieve(expanded_query, k=50)\n   488\t        \n   489\t        # Step 3: \u8bcd\u6c47\u8bed\u4e49\u91cd\u6392\u5e8f\n   490\t        reranked = self.lexical_ranker.rerank(query, candidates, k=20)\n   491\t        \n   492\t        # Step 4: NLI\u8fc7\u6ee4\n   493\t        filtered = self.nli_filter.filter(query, reranked)\n   494\t        \n   495\t        # Step 5: \u77e5\u8bc6\u56fe\u8c31\u5bf9\u9f50\n   496\t        aligned = self.kg_aligner.align(query, filtered)\n   497\t        \n   498\t        # Step 6: \u751f\u6210\u7b54\u6848\n   499\t        answer = self.generator.generate(query, aligned)\n   500\t        \n   501\t        return answer\n   502\t```\n   503\t\n   504\t---\n   505\t\n   506\t## \u7b2c\u4e5d\u8282 SemEval 2024-2025\u8bcd\u6c47\u8bed\u4e49\u4efb\u52a1\n   507\t\n   508\t### \u4e00\u3001SemEval \\u8d85\\u56fe\\u8868\\u793a\\u9886\\u57df\\u6587\\u6863\\n\\n| \\u6307\\u6807 | \\u63d0\\u5347 |\\n|------|------|\\n| \\u4e8b\\u5b9e\\u53ec\\u56de | **+55%** |\\n| \\u54cd\\u5e94\\u6b63\\u786e\\u6027 | **+40%** |\\n| \\u7528\\u6237\\u9a8c\\u8bc1\\u901f\\u5ea6 | **+30%** |\\n\\n### \\u516d\\u3001RAG\\u6574\\u5408\\u67b6\\u6784\\u63a8\\u8350\\n\\n```python\\nclass LexicalSemanticRAG:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\u8bcd\\u6c47\\u8bed\\u4e49\\u589e\\u5f3a\\u7684RAG\\u7cfb\\u7edf\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    def __init__(self):\\n        # 1. Dense Retriever\\n        self.dense_retriever = BGEEmbedding()  # \\u6216 SimCSE\\n        \\n        # 2. Lexical Re-ranker\\n        self.lexical_ranker = BM25WithWordNet()\\n        \\n        # 3. NLI Filter\\n        self.nli_filter = DeBERTaV3MNLI()\\n        \\n        # 4. Knowledge Graph Alignment\\n        self.kg_aligner = ConceptNetLinker()  # \\u6216 UMLS\\n        \\n        # 5. Generator\\n        self.generator = LLM()\\n    \\n    def retrieve_and_generate(self, query):\\n        # Step 1: \\u540c\\u4e49\\u8bcd\\u6269\\u5c55\\n        expanded_query = self.expand_with_wordnet(query)\\n        \\n        # Step 2: Dense\\u68c0\\u7d22\\n        candidates = self.dense_retriever.retrieve(expanded_query, k=50)\\n        \\n        # Step 3: \\u8bcd\\u6c47\\u8bed\\u4e49\\u91cd\\u6392\\u5e8f\\n        reranked = self.lexical_ranker.rerank(query, candidates, k=20)\\n        \\n        # Step 4: NLI\\u8fc7\\u6ee4\\n        filtered = self.nli_filter.filter(query, reranked)\\n        \\n        # Step 5: \\u77e5\\u8bc6\\u56fe\\u8c31\\u5bf9\\u9f50\\n        aligned = self.kg_aligner.align(query, filtered)\\n        \\n        # Step 6: \\u751f\\u6210\\u7b54\\u6848\\n        answer = self.generator.generate(query, aligned)\\n        \\n        return answer\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## \\u7b2c\\u4e5d\\u8282 SemEval 2024-2025\\u8bcd\\u6c47\\u8bed\\u4e49\\u4efb\\u52a1\\n\\n### \\u4e00\\u3001SemEval 2024\\n\\n**Task 1**\\uff1a\\u591a\\u8bed\\u8a00\\u8bed\\u4e49\\u6587\\u672c\\u76f8\\u5173\\u6027\\uff0814\\u79cd\\u975e\\u6d32/\\u4e9a\\u6d32\\u8bed\\u8a00\\uff09\\n- \\u83b7\\u5956\\u7cfb\\u7edfAAdaM\\uff1a\\u673a\\u5668\\u7ffb\\u8bd1\\u6570\\u636e\\u589e\\u5f3a+\\u4efb\\u52a1\\u81ea\\u9002\\u5e94\\u9884\\u8bad\\u7ec3\\n\\n**Task 6 SHROOM**\\uff08Best Task
